1/18/26, 8:43 AM - Peter: Hi everyone
1/18/26, 8:43 AM - David: Hi everyone 👋
1/18/26, 8:45 AM - Amy: There is a dual scoring system where there’s a winner of the week that adds up as the year goes on, as well as a running total of everyone’s score for the year.

Winner of the week gets top points, 2nd gets one less, 3rd gets one less than that and so on. Overall, you want to get the highest score for the weekly score.

The total running score of course you want to get the lowest possible points for. You get a 7 if you miss the day or if you miss the word (i.e. X/6). 

1/18/26, 9:29 AM - Amy added Zara Lai
1/18/26, 9:33 AM - Amy: Hi Zara! I just sent all the background info out before you joined- oops. Will forward them to you separately 🙃
1/18/26, 9:42 AM - Haidee Tang: Hi everyone! 👋🏻
1/18/26, 10:11 AM - Jay: Hi everyone. I’ll explain the scoring rules. Everyone understands the scoring system except for Dave.
1/18/26, 10:12 AM - Jay: Just to confuse Dave even more, I’m changing the scoring a little bit. The main thing to note is that for both competitions, the lower your score, the better!
1/18/26, 10:12 AM - Jay: There are two comps - the overall comp and the weekly comp.
1/18/26, 10:13 AM - Jay: The overall comp is where your daily score is added. Obviously, the lower your overall score, the better.
1/18/26, 10:16 AM - Jay: Then there is the weekly comp. Points are allocated depending on the week’s performance. 

If you come first during the week, you get 1 point. If you come 2nd, you get 2 points etc so, if you come last during the week, you get 8 points (since there are 8 of us). Your score for your position each week is added. Therefore, the lower your score in this comp, the better too.
1/18/26, 10:17 AM - Jay: If you do not submit your Wordle for the day, you get 7 points. 

If you do not guess the Wordle correctly, you are also allocated 7 points.
1/18/26, 10:18 AM - Jay: Btw my week runs from Sunday to Saturday.
1/18/26, 10:22 AM - Carol Tang: Are we starting today then?
1/18/26, 10:23 AM - Jay: We’ll use this week as practice with the comp starting properly next Sunday.
1/18/26, 10:23 AM - Haidee Tang: What happens when there's equal scores for the week?
1/18/26, 10:25 AM - Maggie Aus Tang: Any rules on where you play the wordle or difficulty?




This was only a trial week, but the scores for the week would have been as follows, remembering that 1st place gets 1 point and last place gets 8 points (and shared points for tied positions). 

Remember, the lower, the better:

CZ 33, 7.5 points (finished 7th/8th place, so share the average of 7 and 8 points)
H 32, 6 points
A 31, 5 points
DP 30, 3.5 points
M 29, 2 points
J 27, 1 point

The competition proper starts from tomorrow!
